Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the cycles framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 909. The resulting operator achieves bound O(157/n^4).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the cycles space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 909 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 157. QED.
